# E15-D09 — SCR-026 workspace import / export, preview and rejection states: hi-fi

**Ticket:** E15-D09 (#363) · **Epic:** E15 · **Status:** Draft — text-first; every decision PROPOSED unless RULED (6396085915); boards PENDING (§0: 10 <!-- audit:boards=10 --> boards, 2 <!-- audit:light_twins=2 --> of them light twins, inside the cap of ≤ 10 boards with light twins ≤ 1/3; every other frame is "not drawn — the text is binding; drawn on request"). **Revision:** the whole text, §0 to §16, gated by the tool from its first line to its last; the boards follow in a second PR (§0). **Owner approval pending (#2701)** (agent-delivery adaptations, owner decision 2026-09-25): the owner's `approved` comment or the merge stands in for the ticket's CDO and Security-engineer sign-offs (§13). **Method:** a desk spec over the merged E15-D01, E15-D02, E15-D03, E15-D04 and E15-D06 text and boards, E05-D02 slice 4 and E09-D09 — a heuristic evaluation `(heuristic)`; no session, participant, measured time or sign-off exists and none is invented. **Tool:** `docs/design/_tools/e15_d09_import_export_audit.py` (stdlib, offline, `python3 -I -B`): its `--json` is the only source of every number here, each carrying an `audit:` marker that `--check` re-derives, and its `--self-test` runs 170 <!-- audit:mutations=170 --> in-memory mutations, each caught by exactly one new FAIL.

**Scope** (the ticket): SCR-026, the workspace import — the drop zone (CMP-067) with its visible label and a keyboard path, the file picker, the parse and validation progress, the diff-style preview as a table, every rejection state, the post-import report; the export surface — Export… from SCR-025 and the workspace menus, the pre-export preview with what is included and, first-class, what is excluded, the file name with its format version, the completion; the copy for the trust claims; keyboard, focus and announcements; tokens, the measured contrast, greyscale and CVD; the A, C and G rows. **Out of scope** (the ticket): the file schema itself (`22` `WorkspaceBundle`, E15-S07); rule import / export (SCR-089, E35 / E36: a reuse note only); server-side validation; sharing beyond the file; motion (E15-D14).

**Fixture** (the tool's `FIXTURE` and the contract facts it parses from text; nothing here is typed by hand). Export: the stored workspace Scalping with 2 <!-- audit:fixture.export_layouts=2 --> layouts and 7 <!-- audit:fixture.export_panels=7 --> panels (`D02:511`), saved as `scalping.cv-workspace-v1.json`. Import: `scalping.json`, a colleague's export from a build that knows two more panel kinds, whose Positions pane carries an account filter naming one of the exporter's accounts (its label stays in the tool; no frame, row or line prints it); its preview adds 7 <!-- audit:fixture.panels=7 --> panels and 2 <!-- audit:fixture.layouts=2 --> layouts, drops 3 <!-- audit:fixture.dropped=3 --> items, 2 <!-- audit:fixture.unknown_kinds=2 --> of them unknown kinds, and remaps 1 <!-- audit:fixture.remapped=1 -->. The app reads format version 1 <!-- audit:fixture.app_format=1 -->; the newer file is format version 2 <!-- audit:fixture.newer_format=2 -->, the older one format version 0 <!-- audit:fixture.older_format=0 -->. The budget fixture `wall.json` opens Wall with 12 <!-- audit:fixture.budget_panels=12 --> panels: 11.8 <!-- audit:fixture.measured_ms=11.8 --> ms per frame measured now, about 17.9 <!-- audit:fixture.estimate_ms=17.9 --> ms estimated, against the budget of 16 <!-- audit:contract.budget_ms=16 --> ms p95 (C-14.2). The limits: a file of at most 2 <!-- audit:contract.size_mb=2 --> MB (E15-S07's default, `BL:1136`), checked within 500 <!-- audit:contract.parse_ms=500 --> ms (`14:633`); nesting depth at most 16 <!-- audit:contract.depth=16 --> (`22:19626`); at most 10,000 <!-- audit:contract.nodes=10000 --> values (`BL:1136`; PROPOSED, D09-G5); at most 32 <!-- audit:contract.layouts_max=32 --> layouts a workspace (`22:8725`) and 16 <!-- audit:contract.panes_max=16 --> panes a layout (`D02:133`); at most 32 <!-- audit:contract.workspaces_max=32 --> workspaces a user (`22:8525`); at most 200 <!-- audit:contract.presets_max=200 --> indicator presets a file, whole or not at all (`22:21229–21230`). The caps on a string from the file: names 40 <!-- audit:contract.name_max=40 --> (`22:19638`), symbols 20 <!-- audit:contract.symbol_max=20 --> (`22:19864`), panel kinds 32 <!-- audit:contract.kind_max=32 --> (`22:19848`), drawing labels 120 <!-- audit:contract.label_max=120 --> (`21:3300`), keys 64 <!-- audit:design.key_cap=64 --> and file names 40 <!-- audit:design.file_cap=40 --> (PROPOSED). The import dialog is CMP-043 `size="lg"`, at most 960 <!-- audit:contract.dialog_lg_px=960 --> px wide; the export dialog is `size="md"`, at most 560 <!-- audit:contract.dialog_md_px=560 --> px (`15:463–468`).

**Deviations (recorded, not hidden; each is also a C-row in §12).**

- **Deviation — the deliverable path.** The ticket asks for `docs/design/specs/e15-workspace-import-export.md` (`BL:242`); the spec is this file, beside its E15 siblings (`14:635`), with its gate tool (BQ-1 RULED).
- **Deviation — the branch.** The ticket names `design/e15-workspace-import-export-hifi`; the work is on `design/e15-d09-hifi` (C-4.4; BQ-1 RULED).
- **Deviation — the densities.** The ticket's Included list names densities; `WorkspaceBundle` holds none (`22:21209–21230`; density is global or per panel, `D02:507`), so the Included list names only what the bundle holds and the densities are a C-row (BQ-2 RULED).
- **Deviation — the symbol cap.** BQ-10 RULED reads 16 for symbols from the pattern's `{2,16}`; a symbol is at most 20 characters with its `USDT` (`22:19864`), so the cap here is the contract's 20 and D09-Q1 asks the orchestrator to confirm it; every other number of BQ-10 stands.

| Condition 2 | A1 — the T2 rule                                                                    | Import… is a write, its legs in the server's order, view-as first (`rbac.view_as`), then `rbac.permission`, then `workspace.limit.count`; Export… is never gated; a rejected file never T2-locks Import; an absence is never a lock; validity is never a lock                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | RULED (6396085915) — binding on every frame         |
| Condition 3 | A2 — the gates                                                                      | the tool reads every frame table row by row, every other table and the prose, with case-insensitive vocabulary lists (the gated-exit words, the environment words, the sharing claim, the markup and link words) against whole clauses, never first words; one `--self-test` mutation per ruled sentence and per frame element, S1–S20 the minimum                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | RULED (6396085915) — binding from the first commit  |
| Condition 4 | A3 / A4 — the exits and honesty                                                     | Cancel, Esc, ×, Close and Choose another file are never gated on any frame, view-as and the Viewer's included; merged texts at their merge shas; every departure a PROPOSED A, C or G-row; "RULED" cites 6396085915; no lane is an authority                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | RULED (6396085915) — binding on every frame         |

**Standing (binding on every section).** **A1** T2 is E09-D09's, stated once in §6: Import… is a write and its legs run view-as first; Export… is a read and never gated; a rejection, a refusal, an absence and form validity are never a lock. **A2** every gate reads whole clauses with case-insensitive vocabulary lists, and every ruled sentence and frame element has its mutation. **A3** the exits are never gated — Cancel, `Esc`, ×, Close and Choose another file, on every frame and for every session, view-as and the Viewer's included. **A4** honesty: merged texts at their merge shas, E15-D08 by name only, every departure a PROPOSED A, C or G-row, and nothing measured, signed or drawn is claimed before it is. 16 <!-- audit:bq_rows=16 --> questions and 4 <!-- audit:conditions=4 --> conditions RULED.

## 1. Locked inputs, the trust boundary and decisions

### 1.2 The trust boundary, stated once

This is the one E15 surface where a file crosses the trust boundary: out of the app on export, into it on import (the ticket's Security notes, `BL:266`; E15-S07, `BL:1136`). Each of the ticket's four hazards has one rule here, and every frame of §2–§5 applies it.

| Hazard                                                             | The rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Where it binds                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Exfiltration: the file leaves the machine and may end up in a chat | the server builds the bundle by an allow-list projection (E15-S07, `BL:1136`); no field of `WorkspaceBundle` holds a credential, an account, a trade-group or user id, an environment, an alert or rule binding, P&L, a position or an order (`22:21209–21230`; `22:19838`; C-2.7); the preview names what travels and, first-class, the Excluded list, so a mistake in the build is visible rather than hidden; the client saves only what its lists can show (D09-D2)                | `SCR-025/export-preview`, `SCR-025/export-done` (BQ-2 RULED; owner approval pending (#2701)) |
| Injection through content: every string from the file is untrusted | text only: never markup, never a link, never interpreted (SR-127, `04:965`; C-12.10); capped, an ellipsis past the cap, and nothing past a cap rendered, announced, copied or logged; direction-isolated, each control or bidi character shown as a visible "�" so it never acts (D09-D20); a key named in mono; the report repeats nothing of the file beyond those capped strings; the same rule holds in the applied workspace, whose names the server sanitises again (`22:19626`) | every `caps:` and `renders:` clause; §4.4; §5.5                                              |
| Resource abuse: a file sized or shaped to stall the app            | checked in a worker, cheapest first: the size before a byte is parsed, then the depth and the count of values while it parses, then the schema, the version and the registry, the first failure ending the check; a 2 MB file is checked within 500 ms; a hostile file stalls the worker, never the page or the trading panes behind it                                                                                                                                                | `SCR-026/validating`; §3.4; §5.4                                                             |
| Privilege confusion: a file that would grant or imply access       | an import creates one new workspace, owned by the importer, and changes nothing else; account-scoped elements are dropped and listed, never remapped, and no account the importer cannot see is named; the environment binding is never imported; the new workspace starts unpinned; Import… is a write whose legs run view-as first (§6)                                                                                                                                              | §4.2 rows 3 and 4; `SCR-026/report`; §6                                                      |

**The server re-checks (Condition 1).** No guard in the browser replaces the server's own validation: the server re-checks size, depth, nodes, schema and version when the file is imported (E15-S07), refuses unknown fields on its write paths (SR-040, `04:853`) and treats the browser as hostile (`04:54`; C-2.12), and the same holds for every later write of the workspace, `PUT /workspaces/{workspaceId}` included (the ticket's Security notes, `BL:266`). `SCR-026/validating` says so in its `server:` clause, which the tool gates.

**Nothing before the send, nothing after a refusal (Condition 1; BQ-5 RULED).** No workspace, layout or pane exists before Import as new workspace is pressed on a preview; a refusal at any step keeps nothing of the file; and the import is one request that creates everything or nothing (D09-G1), so no frame of this spec shows a partial import.

**What the user is told (BQ-2 RULED; owner approval pending (#2701)).** Two sentences carry the trust claims, each gated byte for byte wherever it shows: the Excluded list on the export and the trust note on the import (§9 holds both). The caveat says what the file does show; the sharing claim of `22:12679`'s description is written nowhere in this spec (D09-G11).

### 1.4 Decisions of this spec (PROPOSED unless RULED)

Each row is stated in full where it applies; a ruled question is the questions table's and is never repeated here.

| Id      | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Where      |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- |
| D09-D1  | the Included and Excluded lists stand side by side in equal columns, so the Excluded list never sits below a long Included list; at 200 % they stack, the Included list first                                                                                             | §2.3       |
| D09-D2  | before the preview the client reads the fetched bundle with the import's own check: data the lists cannot show, or a setting the import would drop as account-scoped, fails the export closed; a limit an import would refuse keeps Download file and adds a warning line | §2.3       |
| D09-D3  | while the workspace has unsaved changes, a line says the file holds it as last saved                                                                                                                                                                                      | §2.3       |
| D09-D4  | the bytes saved are the response body as fetched, written whole or not at all; the page learns only the file's base name and the server never a path; where a browser has no save picker, the hand-off counts as the save (D09-Q2)                                        | §2.4       |
| D09-D5  | the slug rule in full, the empty name and the Windows device names included (D09-G14)                                                                                                                                                                                     | §2.4       |
| D09-D6  | `workspace.exported` fires when the write ends and carries counts only                                                                                                                                                                                                    | §2.4       |
| D09-D7  | when the bundle arrives, focus moves to the title only while it is still on the Cancel the dialog opened on                                                                                                                                                               | §2.1       |
| D09-D8  | `SCR-025/export-failed` takes three more sentences: a `403` or `404` from the read, a bundle the check declines and a write that failed                                                                                                                                   | §2.1       |
| D09-D9  | the export's preparing line waits for the show-delay BQ-9 rules for the import, 150 ms                                                                                                                                                                                    | §2.1, §3.4 |
| D09-D10 | the native input sits behind Choose file…, clipped out of view and out of the Tab order, and the button labels it                                                                                                                                                         | §3.3       |
| D09-D11 | the trust note is the import dialog's description, heard as focus enters                                                                                                                                                                                                  | §3.2       |
| D09-D12 | the import dialog's top edge stays where it opened while its body grows                                                                                                                                                                                                   | §3.2       |
| D09-D13 | the file line names the chosen file, capped at 40 <!-- audit:design.file_cap=40 --> characters, as text                                                                                                                                                                   | §3.3       |
| D09-D14 | a drop of two or more files reads none of them and speaks "Drop one .json file" once                                                                                                                                                                                      | §3.3       |
| D09-D15 | the value text counts the steps and never names them (D09-C13)                                                                                                                                                                                                            | §3.4       |
| D09-D16 | the import is one request that creates everything or nothing, never `createWorkspace` followed by `createLayout` for each layout (D09-G1)                                                                                                                                 | §3.5, §5.5 |
| D09-D17 | Import as new workspace keeps its label while it is busy, and "Still importing…" shows after CMP-001's slow-path delay                                                                                                                                                    | §3.5       |
| D09-D18 | when the view was closed during the send, a persistent `success` toast with View report, or a persistent `danger` toast with Review, carries the outcome                                                                                                                  | §3.5, §7   |
| D09-D19 | each Item cell is its row's header, and every row stays in the page, never windowed                                                                                                                                                                                       | §4.4, §7   |
| D09-D20 | every string from the file is direction-isolated, and each control or bidi character in it shows as a visible "�" instead of acting                                                                                                                                       | §4.4, §5.5 |
| D09-D21 | Copy report's plain text: one numbered line per row, its cells joined by " · ", no line starting with a value from the file; "Couldn't copy the report." when the clipboard is refused                                                                                    | §4.5       |
| D09-D22 | `layout.imported` carries the file's own `schemaVersion`, as read before any migration                                                                                                                                                                                    | §4.5       |
| D09-D23 | the report of a migrated import carries the migration line above its table                                                                                                                                                                                                | §4.4       |
| D09-D24 | Import anyway is a CMP-001 `primary`, not `danger`, because nothing is destroyed (D09-A9)                                                                                                                                                                                 | §4.4       |
| D09-D25 | `schema_version` is read as a whole number written in decimal; absent, empty or anything else is a schema failure that names the key (D09-G2)                                                                                                                             | §5.3       |
| D09-D26 | the worker counts the nesting and the values as it reads and stops at the first limit crossed, before any document is built                                                                                                                                               | §5.4       |
| D09-D27 | a key or a file name is capped at 64 <!-- audit:design.key_cap=64 --> or 40 characters, and its accessible name carries the same capped text, followed by "(shortened)" when it was cut                                                                                   | §5.5       |
| D09-D28 | `SCR-026/refused-limit`'s footer is Cancel alone, and Cancel takes focus, because another file would meet the same limit                                                                                                                                                  | §5.5       |
| D09-D29 | a `403` at the send turns the dialog's verb T2 where it stands (`SCR-026/import-leg-lost`); no event fires on a refusal at the send                                                                                                                                       | §5.5       |
| D09-D30 | a refusal at the send speaks politely, focus on the frame's named button (D09-C12)                                                                                                                                                                                        | §7         |
| D09-D31 | two more refusals in words: a key present but not allowed, the malformed file's variant, and a store that does not answer at the send, the server refusal's variant                                                                                                       | §5.4, §5.5 |

## 2. Export: entry points, the preview dialog, the file and its completion

Export is a read. `exportWorkspace` returns the stored workspace as one `WorkspaceBundle`, `workspaces:read`, scope `self` (`22:12676–12682`), so Export… is never gated (a read): T1 for every session on a stored workspace, Owner, Manager, Viewer and view-as alike (`D02:645`; T2 is stated once, in §6). It sits in three places and opens one dialog (BQ-7 RULED, §2.2): a CMP-043 `role="dialog"`, `size="md"`, at most 560 <!-- audit:contract.dialog_md_px=560 --> px wide (`15:466`), which calls `exportWorkspace` once, counts the Included list from the bundle it gets and saves that same bundle, so what the owner checks is what the file holds (`D02:511`; F-LAY-6, `FL:139–171`). Beside the Included list stands the Excluded list, first-class (§2.3). The file is written on this machine through the platform's save sheet and its name carries the format version (§2.4); no path ever goes to the server (SR-097, `04:922`), and nothing leaves the machine until the owner sends the file on. The trust copy is BQ-2's ruled text, owner approval pending (#2701), and the sharing claim in `exportWorkspace`'s description is written nowhere in this spec (D09-G11). A decision that names no ruling is PROPOSED.

### 2.2 Entry points and the dialog

**Three places, one dialog (BQ-7 RULED).** Export… sits wherever a workspace is managed, and each place opens the same dialog on the workspace it belongs to:

| Place                     | Control                        | The dialog opens                                                       | Focus when the dialog ends                                 | Source                                                       |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ |
| SCR-025's Share section   | CMP-001 `secondary`, "Export…" | stacked over SCR-025, one more layer (E05-D02 slice 4, conventions 32) | SCR-025's Export…                                          | E15-D08, PENDING by name; if it changes, this row follows it |
| the tab's `[⋯]`           | a CMP-041 item, "Export…"      | over the workspace                                                     | `[⋯]`, the menu's own button (the item went with the menu) | `D03:384`                                                    |
| the rail's workspace menu | a CMP-041 item, "Export…"      | over the workspace                                                     | the rail row's menu button                                 | `D02:511`                                                    |

Each place acts on the stored workspace it belongs to and on nothing else: the file holds that workspace as last saved (§2.3). SCR-025's Share section is E15-D02 §5.10's, its hi-fi E15-D08's (PENDING); this spec adds no shortcut and no palette command, and the three places are the ones BQ-7 RULED names.

**Availability, not T2.** Where the workspace has no stored copy, the viewer's unsaved default (V-4, `D03:583`) and a workspace never stored (`D03:384`), there is nothing to read, so the item keeps its place and its label, the label in `color.text.tertiary` and the item `aria-disabled="true"`, with "This workspace isn't saved." as its second line (`ws.export.unstored`, `D03:625`): no lock glyph, no CMP-079 and no registered reason key, the availability form of E15-D03 §1.4 (`D03:118–120`; `SCR-025/export-unavailable`). The arrows still reach it, and its second line is read with its name.

**The dialog (`15:463–469`).** A CMP-043 `role="dialog"` with `aria-modal="true"` and `size="md"`, titled "Export Scalping" with the stored name, so an unsaved rename is not in the title and a long name wraps rather than being cut, and with its close × named "Close Export Scalping". Opening it calls `exportWorkspace` once and shows "Preparing the file…" until the bundle arrives (`D02:511`); preparing is a load, so its Cancel stays (`15:409–417`). The scrim covers the workspace below the TopBar; the TopBar with its environment badge, the StatusBar and the shell's live regions stay above it (E05-D02 slice 4, conventions 31; `D02:84`). Opened from SCR-025, the dialog is one more layer, and `Esc` ends it alone and returns focus to SCR-025's Export… (H22, `S4:734`). The dialog holds no edits, so Cancel, `Esc`, × and a press on the scrim end it at once, with no prompt (H36, `S4:904`) and nothing exported (`FL:146`).

**Anatomy and redlines (comfortable; compact changes the control heights only).** Density re-points `size.control.height` and `size.row.height` and nothing else (`packages/ui/src/theme/theme.css`), so every other value below holds in both densities. Every binding is a semantic name; light and high contrast re-point the same aliases, so the light twin (board 10) changes no binding, and §8 measures each pair in the three themes.

| Part   | Component and variant                     | Layout and redlines                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Tokens (dark and light, the same names)                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------ | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Scrim  | CMP-043's (conventions 31)                | over the workspace below the TopBar                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `palette.black` at `opacity.scrim` 0.6 <!-- audit:redline.opacity.scrim=0.6 -->                                                                                                                           |
| Card   | CMP-043 `role="dialog"`, `size="md"`      | at most 560 <!-- audit:contract.dialog_md_px=560 --> px wide, as tall as its content up to 80 % of the viewport, past which the body scrolls between the fixed header and footer (`15:466–468`); padding `space.dialog.padding` 24 <!-- audit:redline.space.dialog.padding=24 --> px; header, body and footer `space.stack.md` 12 <!-- audit:redline.space.stack.md=12 --> px apart | `color.surface.overlay`, `elevation.dialog`, `radius.dialog` 10 <!-- audit:redline.radius.dialog=10 --> px, a `border.hairline` 1 <!-- audit:redline.border.hairline=1 --> px `color.border.default` edge |
| Header | CMP-043's header; CMP-002 `ghost` `close` | one row: the title at its start, the × at its end, a square of `size.control.height`, 32 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.comfortable=32 --> px comfortable and 24 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.compact=24 --> px compact, `space.inset.sm` 8 <!-- audit:redline.space.inset.sm=8 --> px from the title; a long title wraps to a second line                                      | the title `type.ui.heading-sm` + `color.text.primary` (`h2`); the × glyph in `color.text.primary`                                                                                                         |

### 2.3 The Included and Excluded lists: anatomy and redlines

**What the two lists say (BQ-2 RULED; owner approval pending (#2701)).** The Included list is counted from the fetched bundle, so it names only what `WorkspaceBundle` holds (`22:21209–21230`): no density, which no workspace stores (the third deviation). The Excluded list is BQ-2's sentence, byte for byte, drawn as a list of its five clauses and first-class: the same type, weight and column width as the Included list, never a footnote, a hint or a tooltip. It holds for every file this dialog saves: the bundle has no field for any of the five classes (`22:21213`; a pane's settings never hold credentials, account ids or user ids, `22:19838`, `21:5069`; credentials never leave the secrets module, C-2.7), the server builds the bundle by copying fields in by name, never by spreading a row (E15-S07, `BL:1136`), and the client saves only a bundle that passes the check below. The caveat says what the file does show, a trader's symbols, layouts and settings, which makes the file user-confidential (E15-X01); the sharing claim of `exportWorkspace`'s description is never written (D09-G11).

| Included item                 | Built from the bundle (`22:21209–21230`)                                                                                                             | For Scalping                                                             | Left out when                     |
| ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------- |
| the layouts, with their names | `layouts[]`, each `name` (at most 40 <!-- audit:contract.name_max=40 --> characters, `22:19638`), in the bundle's order                              | 2 <!-- audit:fixture.export_layouts=2 --> layouts (Layout 1, Order-flow) | the workspace has no layout yet   |
| the panels                    | every pane of every layout, `layouts[].panes[]`                                                                                                      | 7 <!-- audit:fixture.export_panels=7 --> panels                          | no layout holds a panel           |
| the symbols                   | each distinct `symbol` of a pane or of a drawing, in the order first met                                                                             | symbols BTCUSDT, ETHUSDT                                                 | no pane or drawing names a symbol |
| the sync groups               | each distinct `pane_config.sync_group`                                                                                                               | sync groups A and B                                                      | no pane is in a group             |
| the panel settings            | each pane's `pane_config.settings`                                                                                                                   | each panel's settings                                                    | no layout holds a panel           |
| the drawings                  | `drawings[]`, with ", with their text labels" while one carries a `label` (at most 120 <!-- audit:contract.label_max=120 --> code points, `21:3300`) | 12 drawings, with their text labels                                      | there is no drawing               |
| the chart templates           | `chart_templates[]`                                                                                                                                  | 1 chart template                                                         | there is none                     |
| the indicator presets         | `indicator_presets[]`: every live preset of the caller, never truncated (`22:21226–21230`)                                                           | 3 indicator presets                                                      | there is none                     |

A count of one takes the singular ("1 layout (Layout 1)", "1 panel"); the items keep the sentence's order and the last one takes its full stop. The workspace record itself (its name, whether it opens at startup, its icon and its place in the rail) travels as the file's `workspace` (`22:21213–21217`) and is named by the title; the format version and the time of export travel too (`22:21215–21216`), the version also in the file's name (§2.4). Every name in the lists is rendered as text, never as markup (SR-127, `04:965`).

**The check before the preview (PROPOSED).** The preview can vouch only for what it lists, so before it draws the lists the client reads the fetched bundle with the import's own check (§3's five steps, in the same worker as the import's, `14:633`), and the export never saves what the preview cannot show:

- A field outside `WorkspaceBundle`'s schemas, or a pane setting the import would drop as account-scoped (§4.2's third row), fails the export closed: `SCR-025/export-failed` with "Couldn't prepare the file: it holds something this preview can't show. Nothing was exported." `WorkspaceBundle` and `ChartTemplateInput` are not closed today (D09-G4); the server's allow-list projection stays the authority (E15-S07, `BL:1136`) and this check never replaces it (`04:54`): it only declines to save what it cannot list.
- A limit an import would refuse (the file's size, its nesting or its count of values, or its presets: an export carries every live preset, while an import takes at most 200 <!-- audit:contract.presets_max=200 --> of them, `22:21226–21230`) keeps Download file and adds a CMP-028 `warning` line under the caveat: the import's own first sentence, then "You can save it, but this app won't import it." For a file with 240 presets: "This file has 240 indicator presets; a file may carry at most 200. You can save it, but this app won't import it." The limits are E15-S07's until the contract carries them (D09-G5).

**Unsaved changes (PROPOSED).** `exportWorkspace` reads the store, so a layout's unsaved working copy and SCR-025's unsaved edits are not in the file. While the workspace has unsaved changes, a CMP-028 `info` line opens the body: "Unsaved changes aren't in the file: it holds Scalping as last saved." Saving first sends them; a session that cannot save exports what is stored.

**Anatomy and redlines (comfortable / compact).** The body of the preview, the done step and the failed step; §2.2 holds the chrome.

| Part                            | Component and variant                                                                                                                                                              | Layout and redlines (comfortable / compact)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Tokens (dark and light, the same names)                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Unsaved-changes line            | CMP-028 `info`, its title line alone, `live="off"`, only while the workspace has unsaved changes                                                                                   | full width at the top of the body, `space.stack.md` 12 <!-- audit:redline.space.stack.md=12 --> px above the lists; a `border.thick` 2 <!-- audit:redline.border.thick=2 --> px start edge; `radius.card` 6 <!-- audit:redline.radius.card=6 --> px; CMP-028's own padding | words `type.ui.body-strong` + `color.text.primary` on `color.status.info.subtle`; edge and glyph `color.status.info.strong`                                    |
| The two lists                   | one `dl` holding two CMP-036 KeyValueRows                                                                                                                                          | two equal columns `space.inset.xl` 24 <!-- audit:redline.space.inset.xl=24 --> px apart, both from the top of the body; in the reflow state one column, the Included list first                                                                                            | as their parts                                                                                                                                                 |
| Lead-in                         | each CMP-036's `label`: "The file includes:", and the Excluded sentence's opening words up to its colon                                                                            | one line, `space.inset.sm` 8 <!-- audit:redline.space.inset.sm=8 --> px above its first item                                                                                                                                                                               | `type.ui.body-strong` + `color.text.primary`                                                                                                                   |
| Items                           | each CMP-036's `value`: a `ul` named by its lead-in (`aria-labelledby`), one `li` per clause, each keeping the clause's own punctuation, so the list read in order is the sentence | each item at least `size.row.height` tall, 32 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.comfortable=32 --> px comfortable and 24 <!-- audit:redline.size.row.height=24 --> px compact, its words centred in it; a long item wraps inside its column                                  | `type.ui.body` + `color.text.primary` on `color.surface.overlay`                                                                                               |
| Caveat                          | a `p`                                                                                                                                                                              | full width, `space.stack.md` under the columns                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `type.ui.body` + `color.text.secondary`                                                                                                                        |
| Import-limit line               | CMP-028 `warning`, its title line alone, `live="off"`, only while an import would refuse the file                                                                                  | full width, `space.stack.md` under the caveat; the edge, the radius and the padding of the unsaved-changes line                                                                                                                                                            | words `type.ui.body-strong` + `color.text.primary` on `color.status.warning.subtle`; edge and glyph `color.status.warning.strong`                              |
| Completion line (the done step) | CMP-028 `success`, its title line alone, `role="status"`                                                                                                                           | full width at the top of the body, the Excluded row `space.stack.md` under it; the edge, the radius and the padding of the unsaved-changes line                                                                                                                            | words `type.ui.body-strong` + `color.text.primary` on `color.status.success.subtle`; edge and glyph `color.status.success.strong`; the file name `type.num.sm` |
| Failure (the failed step)       | CMP-027 `inline`, `severity` error, `live="polite"`                                                                                                                                | the message full width, its glyph `size.icon` 16 <!-- audit:redline.size.icon=16 --> px before the words                                                                                                                                                                   | glyph `color.status.danger.strong`; words `type.ui.body` + `color.text.primary`                                                                                |

**Dark, light and high contrast.** The three themes re-point the same names (`packages/ui/tokens/themes.json`), and §8 measures every pair from the token files. The lists' words are the dialog's first pair, `color.text.primary` on `color.surface.overlay`, body text in every theme, and the availability label's `color.text.tertiary` passes as text in every theme too. In light, the completion line's edge and glyph on their fill are the faintest pair this section draws that still passes as non-text, so the line's words carry it; and the failure glyph's `color.status.danger.strong` and the import-limit glyph's `color.status.warning.strong` share one greyscale value (§8's greyscale rows), so colour never tells them apart: the two never show together, and each is read by its words. Under `forced-colors: active` the card keeps its `border.hairline` 1 <!-- audit:redline.border.hairline=1 --> px edge and every callout keeps its glyph and its words (E05-D02 slice 4, conventions 38).

### 2.4 The file name and the completion

**The name (BQ-8 RULED; the slug rule is D09-G14).** `<slug>.cv-workspace-v<schema_version>.json`, for the fixture `scalping.cv-workspace-v1.json`. The format token follows SCR-089's `cv-rule-bundle/1` family (`E36D03:153–155`); the version is the bundle's own `schema_version` (`22:21215`), never an app version; and there is no date, because `exported_at` is inside the file (`22:21215–21216`) and a date in the name invites stale copies. The import reads the version inside the file and never the name (§5), so a renamed file imports as well as any other. The slug, PROPOSED in full:

1. the stored name, Unicode NFKD with its combining marks removed, lower-cased;
2. every character outside `a`–`z` and `0`–`9` becomes `-`, a run of them one `-`, and the ends are trimmed;
3. cut at 40 <!-- audit:contract.name_max=40 --> characters, the name's own cap (`22:19638`), and trimmed again;
4. an empty result is `workspace`, and a Windows device name (`con`, `prn`, `aux`, `nul`, `com1` to `com9`, `lpt1` to `lpt9`) takes `workspace-` in front;
5. the version keeps `0`–`9`, `a`–`z` and `.` only, by the same rule (D09-G2: the contract does not yet say what the string holds).

| Stored name                                                                           | File name                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| Scalping                                                                              | `scalping.cv-workspace-v1.json`                             |
| Fanout review — EU session: BTCUSDT, ETH (E15-D03's 40-character name, `D03:126–128`) | `fanout-review-eu-session-btcusdt-eth.cv-workspace-v1.json` |
| Café — Asia open                                                                      | `cafe-asia-open.cv-workspace-v1.json`                       |
| ★★★                                                                                   | `workspace.cv-workspace-v1.json`                            |
| CON                                                                                   | `workspace-con.cv-workspace-v1.json`                        |

**The save sheet and the write.** Download file hands the file to the platform's save sheet, the operating system's file dialog reached through the browser's save picker or the desktop shell, with the suggested name and the JSON type (`application/json`, `.json`). The sheet is the operating system's layer, not this dialog's: the owner picks the folder and may change the name, and the page learns only the chosen file's base name, never its folder. The bytes are the response body as fetched, the very bytes the lists were counted from, never re-serialised and never added to: plain JSON, which E15-S07 keeps so a person can read the file before sending it (`BL:1136`). They are written whole or not at all (to a temporary file, then renamed), so a failed write leaves no partial file and turns the dialog to `SCR-025/export-failed` with "Couldn't save the file. Nothing was exported." (PROPOSED), whose Try again opens the sheet again. A sheet closed unsaved returns to the preview as it was, `SCR-025/export-cancelled`: nothing saved and no event (`FL:146`). Where a browser has no save picker, the file goes to its downloads under the suggested name; the hand-off is the only answer the page gets there, so it counts as the save (PROPOSED; D09-Q2). No path, folder or file name ever goes to the server (SR-097, `04:922`).

**The completion step (BQ-8 RULED).** When the write ends, the body changes in place (`SCR-025/export-done`): a CMP-028 `success` line, "Exported Scalping as scalping.cv-workspace-v1.json.", the file name in `type.num.sm` and selectable, then the Excluded list, unchanged, so the owner sees what the file leaves out as they send it on. The Included list, the caveat and the conditional lines leave; the footer is Close alone, and focus lands on it. The line is spoken once, politely (CMP-028 `success`, `role="status"`, `15:311–320`). The name is the base name the sheet returned, so a file the owner renamed reads "Exported Scalping as desk-copy.json.", shown whole, as text, wrapping anywhere and never with its folder. Close, `Esc` or × ends the dialog, and focus returns to the opener (§2.2).

**The event.** `workspace.exported{panelCount, schemaVersion}` fires once the file is saved: when the write ends, or, under the browser fallback, at the hand-off that stands for the save. The press of Download file alone never fires it, nor does a sheet closed unsaved or a failure. It carries the counts the lists showed: `panelCount` 7 <!-- audit:fixture.export_panels=7 --> and `schemaVersion` "1" for the fixture. It is analytics, never audit (`D02:84`), and it carries no workspace name, file name or symbol (`21:5069`); SCR-025's catalogue entry lists it on its Audit line (`14:613–624`), which D09-G10 settles.

### 2.5 Per frame: what changes, the components and the focus

Each row is a frame of §2.1, in the order the flow reaches it; §2.2's chrome and §2.3's anatomy hold unless the row says otherwise.

| State                                      | Reached when                                                                                              | What changes (components and variants)                                                                    | Focus, keys and announcements                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| entry (`SCR-025/export-entry`)             | a menu opens on a stored workspace, or SCR-025 shows its Share section                                    | a CMP-041 item, or SCR-025's CMP-001 `secondary` button                                                   | the arrows reach the item; `Enter` opens the dialog and the menu folds away; nothing is announced beyond the item's name                                                                                                                 |
| unavailable (`SCR-025/export-unavailable`) | the workspace has no stored copy                                                                          | the label in `color.text.tertiary` and its second line; no glyph                                          | the arrows reach it; its name and its second line are read together; `Enter` and `Space` change nothing                                                                                                                                  |
| preparing (`SCR-025/export-preparing`)     | Export… chosen                                                                                            | the dialog, CMP-038 in its body after the show-delay                                                      | focus on Cancel; CMP-038 speaks "Preparing the file…" once; Cancel, `Esc` and × end the read with nothing exported and focus back on the opener                                                                                          |
| preview (`SCR-025/export-preview`)         | the bundle passed the check (§2.3)                                                                        | CMP-038 leaves; the two lists, the caveat and any conditional line arrive; Download file joins the footer | focus moves to the title if it was still on Cancel, and the title is read with the two lists, its `aria-describedby`; `Enter` on Download file opens the save sheet; Cancel, `Esc` and × end the dialog with nothing exported (`FL:146`) |
| failed (`SCR-025/export-failed`)           | the read answered `503` or nothing, or `403` or `404`; the check declined the bundle; or the write failed | CMP-027 `inline` takes the body; Try again joins the footer                                               | focus on Try again; the message is spoken once, politely; Try again reads again (the preparing line, focus on Cancel) or, after a failed write, opens the sheet again                                                                    |
| cancelled (`SCR-025/export-cancelled`)     | the save sheet closed unsaved                                                                             | nothing: the preview as it was                                                                            | focus on Download file; nothing is announced and no event fires                                                                                                                                                                          |
| done (`SCR-025/export-done`)               | the write ended                                                                                           | the completion line over the Excluded list; the footer Close alone                                        | focus on Close; the completion line is spoken once, politely; `workspace.exported` fires                                                                                                                                                 |
| reflow (`SCR-025/export-reflow-200`)       | 200 % zoom on 1280 × 800 (`05:131`)                                                                       | CMP-043's reflow state; the two lists stack                                                               | as at 100 %, with nothing clipped                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

**Announcements.** Each spoken line comes from a region mounted empty with the dialog, its text inserted when its state begins, and each is spoken once, politely (`05:144`, `05:150`); opening the dialog adds nothing to what the focused control and the preparing line say, and a dismissed save sheet says nothing at all.

**Exits.** Cancel, `Esc`, × and Close end the export dialog on each of its frames and for every session, view-as included, and `Esc` closes the menu that holds Export… (§7 states the rule once). Before the write they end it with nothing exported; a write already started still completes whole or not at all (§2.4). Focus returns to the opener, or, when the opener has gone, to the host's named fallback and never to the page's `body` (`15:466–468`).

## 3. The import flow: entry, the dialog, choosing a file, validating, applying

SCR-026 is one CMP-043 `role="dialog"`, `size="lg"`, at most 960 <!-- audit:contract.dialog_lg_px=960 --> px wide (`15:466`), because the preview's four columns need the width (BQ-9 RULED). Its steps share one chrome — choose, validating, preview, the send, the report — so the title "Import workspace", the close × and the footer's place never move while the body changes; no wizard header and no step counter is drawn. The import only ever creates a new workspace and never writes over one (D-30, `D02:148`; `D02:522`), so it needs no SCR-029 and no discard prompt. The trust note, `D02:513` verbatim, sits under the title from the first step to the send (BQ-2 RULED). Nothing is created before Import as new workspace, and nothing of the file is kept after a Cancel or a refusal (Condition 1; BQ-5 RULED). The frames below are binding and the tool gates them row by row; §3.2 to §3.5 give each one its anatomy, redlines, tokens and focus, and a decision that names no ruling is PROPOSED. The preview and the report are §4, the rejections and refusals §5, the T2 rule §6, and the full keyboard and announcement walk §7.

### 3.2 The entries and the dialog's one chrome

**Entries (BQ-9 RULED).** Import… is an item of the tab strip's "+" menu, after New workspace… and Open workspace… (`D03:365`), and of the rail's workspace menu; either opens the dialog empty (`D02:513`), routed as `?modal=import` (BQ-9's route). Import… is a write, because it creates a workspace (`createWorkspace`, `22:8521`; no operation takes a bundle yet, `22:21226`), so §6's legs gate it, view-as first, with the keys merged E15-D02 and E15-D03 carry (`D02:655`; `D03:564`). An Owner or a Manager under 32 <!-- audit:contract.workspaces_max=32 --> workspaces (`22:8525`) meets `SCR-026/entry` (`04:1139`); the three other entry frames, `SCR-026/entry-view-as`, `SCR-026/entry-permission` and `SCR-026/entry-limit`, are drawn as §6 states the rule. Each menu's own `Esc` closes it and returns focus to its button, on every entry frame (§7).

**One chrome, five steps (BQ-9 RULED).** The body changes with the step; the header, the trust note and the footer's place do not. A rejection or a refusal takes the preview's place in the body (§5).

| Step        | The body, top to bottom                                                                                                                     | The footer, Cancel first and the primary last | Frames                                                                             |
| ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| choose      | the trust note; the drop zone around Choose file…; the help line (§3.3)                                                                     | Cancel                                        | `SCR-026/pick`, `SCR-026/drag-over`, `SCR-026/drag-over-rejected`                  |
| validating  | the trust note; the zone; the file line; CMP-023 (§3.4)                                                                                     | Cancel                                        | `SCR-026/validating`                                                               |
| preview     | the trust note; the zone; the file line; the migration or the budget line when one applies; the clash line when one applies; CMP-049 (§4.4) | Cancel · Import as new workspace              | `SCR-026/preview`, `SCR-026/preview-migrated`, `SCR-026/preview-over-budget`       |
| the send    | the trust note; the preview's lines and table, read-only (§3.5)                                                                             | Close · Import as new workspace, busy         | `SCR-026/budget-confirm` (stacked first, over the budget only), `SCR-026/creating` |
| the report  | the report region (§4.5)                                                                                                                    | Copy report · Close                           | `SCR-026/report`                                                                   |
| a rejection | the trust note; CMP-027 `inline`; the zone with Choose file…, its words and its help line (§5.2)                                            | Cancel · Choose another file                  | §5.1's six rejections and two refusals                                             |

**Anatomy and redlines (comfortable; compact changes the control and row heights only).** One semantic name per binding: the light twin (board 09) re-points the same names (`16` §4) and changes none, and §8 measures every pair in dark, light and high contrast. Every px value below is the tool's, from the token files.

| Part       | Component and variant                                                            | Layout and redlines                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Tokens                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Scrim      | CMP-043's                                                                        | over the page between the TopBar and the StatusBar, which stay above it with the shell's live regions (`15:463–468`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `palette.black` at `opacity.scrim` 0.6 <!-- audit:redline.opacity.scrim=0.6 -->                                                                                                                                       |
| Card       | CMP-043 `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="true"`, `size="lg"`                        | at most 960 <!-- audit:contract.dialog_lg_px=960 --> px wide (`15:466`); as tall as its content, then CMP-043's scrolls state with a fixed header and footer; its top edge stays where it opened while the body grows, so the title and the zone never move under the pointer (PROPOSED); padding `space.dialog.padding` 24 <!-- audit:redline.space.dialog.padding=24 --> px; header, body and footer `space.stack.md` 12 <!-- audit:redline.space.stack.md=12 --> px apart | `color.surface.overlay`; `elevation.dialog`; `radius.dialog` 10 <!-- audit:redline.radius.dialog=10 --> px; a `border.hairline` 1 <!-- audit:redline.border.hairline=1 --> px `color.border.default` edge, decorative |
| Header     | the title, an `h2` and the dialog's `aria-labelledby`; CMP-002 `ghost` for the × | one row: the title at its start, the × at its end in a square of the control height, 32 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.comfortable=32 --> px comfortable and 24 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.compact=24 --> px compact; the × named "Close Import workspace"                                                                                                                                                                                                             | title `type.ui.heading-sm` + `color.text.primary`; the × glyph in its context's text token (`15:225`)                                                                                                                 |
| Trust note | CMP-028 `info`, `role="note"`, `live="off"` (E36-D03's privacy-note form)        | under the header, full width; the dialog's `aria-describedby`, so a screen reader hears it as focus enters the dialog (PROPOSED); never a Tab stop; padding `space.inset.sm` 8 <!-- audit:redline.space.inset.sm=8 --> px; a `border.thick` 2 <!-- audit:redline.border.thick=2 --> px start edge; the glyph at `size.icon` 16 <!-- audit:redline.size.icon=16 --> px                                                                                                        | fill `color.status.info.subtle`; edge and glyph `color.status.info.strong`; words `type.ui.body` + `color.text.primary`; `radius.card` 6 <!-- audit:redline.radius.card=6 --> px                                      |
| Body       | the step's content (§3.3 to §4.5)                                                | `space.stack.md` 12 <!-- audit:redline.space.stack.md=12 --> px between its blocks; it scrolls only at 200 % zoom, where the table stacks (`SCR-026/reflow-200`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Footer     | a CMP-001 row                                                                    | end-aligned, `space.inset.sm` 8 <!-- audit:redline.space.inset.sm=8 --> px apart, Cancel (or Close) first and the primary last, each the control height                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | primary `color.action.primary.default` + `color.text.on-action`; secondary `color.action.secondary.default` + `color.text.primary`                                                                                    |
| Focus      | CMP-039                                                                          | a `border.focus` 2 <!-- audit:redline.border.focus=2 --> px ring at CMP-039's offset on every Tab stop                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `color.focus.ring`                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

**Focus and keys in the dialog (BQ-13 RULED; the walk itself is §7 and board 08).** Opening moves focus to Choose file… (CMP-043's `initialFocusRef`, `15:463–468`); nothing is announced beyond what focus entering reads, the title and then the trust note as the dialog's description. `Tab` runs the trap in DOM order: the ×, Choose file…, the table's scroll region once a preview stands, then the footer from its first button to the primary. The drop zone is never a Tab stop, and neither are the trust note, the help line and the file line, which are text. `Esc` closes the topmost layer only: the OS file picker first (the browser's own layer, which returns focus to Choose file…), then the stacked confirm, then the dialog (`S4:734`). Before the send, `Esc`, Cancel, the × and a press on the scrim close the dialog with nothing imported (`SCR-026/cancelled`); once the file is sent they close the view and cancel nothing (`15:468`). On close, focus returns to the button of the menu Import… came from, "+" or the rail's workspace menu, because the item went with its menu (CMP-043's named fallback, `15:463–468`); after a successful import, Close lands on the new workspace's tab (§4.5).

### 3.3 Choose file… and the drop zone (CMP-067)

**Choose file… (BQ-13 RULED; AC 6).** A real `<button>`, CMP-001 `secondary`, whose label "Choose file…" is the visible label `14:632` asks for and whose `Enter` or `Space` opens the OS file picker. It fronts CMP-067's native `<input type="file">` (`15:690–696`), which carries `accept=".json,application/json"` and `multiple` false: the input sits behind the button, clipped out of view and out of the Tab order (`tabindex="-1"`), and is labelled by the button (`aria-labelledby`), so the one control a keyboard or screen-reader user meets is the button (PROPOSED anatomy; E36-D03's SCR-089 puts the native input first, `E36D03:153–155`). The button's `aria-describedby` names the drop hint, then the help line, so a screen reader hears "Choose file…, button, or drop a workspace file here, JSON, up to 2 MB". The OS picker filters by `accept`; leaving it with `Esc` returns focus to the button with nothing chosen and nothing announced. Choose file… stays live through the validating, the preview and every rejection: a new pick discards the file in hand and starts the check again from its first step.

**The drop zone (BQ-9 RULED; `05:35`, `05:101`).** A box around Choose file… that adds a second way in and is never the only one (`15:694`; WCAG 2.5.7). The drop zone has no role and no name of its own and is never a Tab stop; its words, "or drop a workspace file here" (`D02:513`), are the button's description. A drop only adds to the input: a dropped file goes through the same five steps as a picked one (§3.4), and the zone takes one file. While files are dragged over it, the zone shows one of two states, each in words and an edge, never colour alone:

- `SCR-026/drag-over`, one file that may be JSON: the words "Drop to check the file" in place of the hint, the edge `color.border.selected` at `border.thick` 2 <!-- audit:redline.border.thick=2 --> px inside the box (no layout shift) on the fill `color.surface.hover`.
- `SCR-026/drag-over-rejected`, two or more files, or one whose type the drag already shows is not JSON: the words "Drop one .json file", the edge `color.border.default`. A drag carries no file name, so neither state prints one.

Dropping two or more files reads none of them and changes nothing: the zone returns to rest and its drag-over words, "Drop one .json file", are announced once, politely (PROPOSED; no event fires, because nothing was read). Dropping one file that is not JSON leads to `SCR-026/rejected-wrong-type` (§5.1): the picker filters by type, a drop does not. A drag is a pointer gesture and its states announce nothing; Choose file… is the keyboard path to the same input (`05:101`).

| Part         | Component and variant                                                                                               | Layout and redlines                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Tokens                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The zone     | CMP-067's drop target, a box with no role (PROPOSED: the label, the hint and the states are A-rows to CMP-067, §10) | full body width; padding `space.inset.xl` 24 <!-- audit:redline.space.inset.xl=24 --> px; Choose file… centred, its words `space.stack.md` 12 <!-- audit:redline.space.stack.md=12 --> px under it; `radius.card` 6 <!-- audit:redline.radius.card=6 --> px; a `border.hairline` 1 <!-- audit:redline.border.hairline=1 --> px solid edge, never dashed: `color.border.dashed` is not a shipped token (`15:695`) and E36-D03 re-bound it the same way (`E36D03:272`) | edge `color.border.default`, decorative because the zone is not the control (§8); the words `type.ui.body` + `color.text.secondary`                                               |
| Choose file… | CMP-001 `secondary`, `md`                                                                                           | the control height, 32 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.comfortable=32 --> px comfortable and 24 <!-- audit:redline.size.control.compact=24 --> px compact; the dialog's focus ring                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | fill `color.action.secondary.default`; label `color.text.primary`                                                                                                                 |
| Native input | `<input type="file">` (CMP-067)                                                                                     | clipped out of view; `tabindex="-1"`; `aria-labelledby` the button                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | none drawn                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Help line    | text under the zone                                                                                                 | `space.stack.md` 12 <!-- audit:redline.space.stack.md=12 --> px under it; "JSON, up to 2 MB" (`e15.import.help`)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `type.ui.caption` + `color.text.secondary`                                                                                                                                        |
